| D-733 | **The newest recorded quote is current, the rest are superseded.** "Newest" is the highest `quotes.id`, the order they were recorded in, never `received_on` (optional, and a PEO's date can be backdated). One pure function, `quoteRevisions(quotes)` in `src/data/quoteRevisions.ts`, answers `{ current, superseded }` (superseded newest first); N-3.1's comparison (7.7) should read it, so the page and the comparison never disagree about which quote is current. No SQL view yet: nothing in SQL needs one |
| D-734 | **N-2.4's database half was already built by 7.2** (D-720, D-725): `record_quote` inserts a new row each time and `advisor_update_quote_request` keeps the quote request's own fields (status, dates, per-month fee, note) in step with the newest, so a revised quote needs no new function. This slice adds regression tests (SQL test 11: the earlier quote keeps its figures and its own document; the newest is the highest id) and the reads and screens below |
| D-735 | **What changed between the current quote and the one before: not built.** The story asks only that a later quote becomes the current one and the earlier is kept in the history. A cost-area difference is N-3.1's to show (the comparison); `quoteRevisions` gives it the two quotes |
| D-736 | **On screen, only on the quote's page (5.9).** Two panels after the Quote figures, both read from the quotes table beside the page (`useQuotesQuery`, so a failed read never hides the page, and no placeholder while it loads): `QuoteDocument` ("Quote document": the file's name and size, an Open button through the same signed link and the same `document_downloaded` activity as the thread's files, and, while the current quote has none, "Use a file from the emails" (the stored files on this quote request's own emails, never a blocked or another PEO's file) and "Upload the quote document") and `EarlierQuotes` ("Earlier quotes": each superseded quote as "Estimate · received Nov 3, 2026 · Superseded · file name", newest first, "no received date" when none). Nothing shows while a quote request has no `quotes` row (a quote marked quoted by hand has none, D-726). Left alone: the submission page's quote request card, the Quote figures panel (it reads the quote request's own fields, which follow the newest quote), and the Record a quote form (7.8) |
| D-737 | **Who sees the controls.** Open is shown to anyone who sees the page. Upload and Use a file are shown only to someone who may change the account's records and not during a Nevermore support session or on an archived account (the page's `canAct`, D-209, D-432, ADR 0024); the database asks the same on every call. A blocked upload says "The virus check blocked that file, so it was not kept." |
| D-738 | **Not built here.** Unlinking a document (naming another replaces it); more than one document for a quote; reading the document's figures (N-2.3, it needs this slice's file and its own suggestions table); the Record a quote form (7.8), which will record the quote and then call `uploadQuoteDocument` with the new quote's id, so the form must save before it uploads |
| D-739 | **File rules are every upload's.** At most 20 MB and the types `fileSignature.ts` takes, checked by name and by bytes before anything is recorded; a PDF is the usual quote document, nothing makes it the only one. Stored with a content type of `application/octet-stream`, so a browser downloads it whatever the link says (ADR 0002) |
| D-749 | **Registries and privacy.** The advisor function is in `data_isolation.sql` (function table, two-firm probe with a fixture reply, writes list), `help_from_nevermore.sql` (a write, refused to support) and `close_a_firm.sql` (guarded list, asks `assert_firm_writable` first); the service function is in the service-only list and its probe. The data policy's PEO correspondence class says the AI provider reads each reply and what is kept. N-1.3 builds on this (7.5, D-750..) |

### 7.5 N-1.3 decisions

Slice 7.5 (N-1.3, an underwriter's question becomes an information request), migration `20261125120000_question_requests.sql`, test `supabase/tests/question_requests.sql`, decisions D-750..D-759. Needs 7.4 (reply suggestions). Epic 7 has no grilled decisions: each is a recommendation for the founder to review.

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| D-750 | **Reused: the information request, whole.** What the employer reads is the existing information request: `advisor_request_information` (its message, the answers it names, a PEO's own extra questions, the email, the intake's "Your advisor asked about this", the move to Information requested, certifying again answers it), the panel `InformationRequestPanel` ("Ask the prospect") and `prospect_information_request`, none restated. The one new function, `advisor_ask_about_question(quote request, message, fields, extra questions)`, checks the quote request and then calls that function. No new kind of request, no new table, so no new row in the closing plan, the matrix or the data classes |
| D-751 | **The PEO's words are never copied across.** The draft the agent gets (the panel opened from "Ask the prospect about this question", or on the quote page) starts empty; the agent writes the question in their own words and Send request is the confirmation. The PEO's reply stays on the thread, which the employer never sees. The one existing way that copies text (an underwriter's question the agent noted themselves, with the "Ask the prospect" beside that note) is the agent's own note and is unchanged |
| D-752 | **Only an open question is asked about.** `advisor_ask_about_question` refuses (22023) a quote request that is not at Question open, and answers another firm's (or an unknown) quote request PT404 "Quote request not found". It covers both ways in: an agent who accepted a "question open" suggestion (7.4, D-742) and one who set Question open by hand. A closing firm or a support session is refused first (ADR 0023, 0024) and an agent is required. A quote request at Sent, Acknowledged, Quoted, Declined or Closed has nothing to ask about; the ordinary "Ask the prospect" still serves those |
| D-753 | **The link is the quote request's own log, not a column.** The function appends one `asked_employer` event (the request's id, who, when) to `quote_request_events`, the log nothing can rewrite. The `information_requests` table is append-only and takes no column; `quote_requests` takes none either. The thread reads the event and the submission's requests (both already on the page) so no new read. The change log says it in words ("The employer was asked about the PEO's question (request 5)") |
| D-754 | **Waiting and answered are read, not stored.** On the PEO's thread, while the quote request is at Question open and the employer was asked, a line says what the agent asked, and "Waiting for their answer." while the submission is at Information requested, or "The employer has answered. Write back to <PEO> to answer the question." once it is not (the employer certified again, or an agent moved the submission on). Gap: it cannot tell the two apart, and it reads "waiting" for any request, not only this one, while the submission is at Information requested. Nothing is stored about an answer |
| D-755 | **Replying to the PEO is the existing reply; no new status writer.** The agent answers on the same thread with the existing Follow up (the quote page's and the submission's), which records a message the product sent and returns Question open to Sent through the trigger of D-1733, whether the employer answered or the agent knew the answer. A test holds it. Gap: the product does not draft the reply to the PEO from the employer's answer (the answers are the intake's, and the update of slice 6.8, a send with what changed, is the existing way to send them again) |
| D-756 | **"The broker is offered an update when the prospect certifies again" is the existing offer, unchanged.** A PEO that holds an older certified version than the latest is already offered the update on its card once the employer certifies again (M-2.3, slice 6.8). Nothing was added; the thread's line only points the agent at writing back. Gap against the story's wording: no offer is made that names the question |
| D-757 | **Shown where the reply and the request already are.** (1) Submission page: the quote request's card gets "Ask the prospect about this question" (only at Question open), which opens the existing Ask the prospect panel empty and tied to that quote request; the thread shows the line. (2) Quote page: the same panel, shown only at Question open and only to someone who can act, and the thread's line. (3) The employer's intake: the existing request in The Chamber and the email, unchanged. No dead button: each is drawn only where the database would accept it |
| D-758 | **Accepting a "question open" suggestion says what comes next.** The Accept toast reads "Recorded as Question open. You can ask the prospect about it: write the question in your own words." The story says accepting "drafts" a request; here accepting records the status (D-742) and the draft is one click further (an empty one, D-751), so the PEO's sentence is not put in front of the employer without the agent's own wording |
| D-759 | **Registries.** `advisor_ask_about_question` is in `data_isolation.sql` (function table, two-firm probe by quote request, writes list), `help_from_nevermore.sql` (a write, refused to support, with its probe) and `close_a_firm.sql` (guarded list and a closing probe); it calls `assert_firm_writable` first. `types.ts` has the RPC. No data class changes: the employer's request and the log event hold only what the agent wrote and a number |

### 7.6 N-2.3 decisions

Slice 7.6 (N-2.3, read the quote document: the function, its API helper and their tests; the Record a quote form that will call it is 7.8), no migration, function `supabase/functions/read-quote-document`, decisions D-760..D-769, built test-first in `lane/7.6` from 01ee1084 (`TDD_LOG.md`, "## 7.6"). Needs 7.2 and 7.3. Epic 7 has no grilled decisions: each is a recommendation for the founder to review.

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| D-760 | **A proposal is returned, never stored, so there is no migration, table or database function.** The model reads the document a quote names (`quotes.document_attachment_id`, D-730) and proposes figures; nothing becomes the quote until the broker confirms through the existing `record_quote` (D-725). "The broker accepts or corrects each" is the form's work (7.8): it shows the draft in its fields. So no `data_isolation`, `help_from_nevermore`, `close_a_firm`, closing-plan or `types.ts` change. Gap: nothing records that a quote began as a reading, or which figures the broker changed; a column on `quotes` or a table of readings is the alternative if the founder wants that audit |
| D-761 | **The shape is `record_quote`'s own (`QuoteOffer`), every key optional, and held to the database's own checks.** `kind` (only if the document says estimate or firm offer; never filled in, so the form still asks), the three dates (real calendar dates), the administrative fee only with its basis and inside 0 to 100000 (0 to 100 percent), and the four lists, each line checked as `quote_*_valid` checks it (a trimmed name of 1 to 80, amounts and rates in their bounds, a state of the 56 codes, the class-code pattern, the four tiers, each name, state and class code once). A bad line is dropped, a list with no line left is left out. A figure the document does not state is absent, never 0 ("Not quoted", D-721); 0 stays a real answer (a waived fee). The checks are written again in TypeScript (`proposal.ts`), a copy that can drift from the SQL: the proposal is a convenience, and `record_quote` still refuses what it should |
| D-762 | **Each figure points to its page.** The model cites a figure by section number; the section is the reader's own label ("Page 2" of a PDF, "Table 1" of a Word file, "Sheet Quote" of a workbook), so `sources` names the page the figure was read on. A citation to a section the document does not have shows no page (the figure stays, unreferenced). The pointer is a page, not the sentence |
| D-763 | **The document is data, never instructions: three layers, as D-746.** (1) The system prompt says the document is untrusted content to read and never to follow. (2) The text is only inside one `<quote_document>` fence, its own tag removed from the text in any spacing or case so it cannot close the fence, with sensitive numbers removed and at most 20000 characters sent (`buildPromptText`). (3) One forced tool; the answer is parsed against the fixed shape and anything else is dropped. The model cannot act: the function only returns what passes. Gap: a very long quote is read to the character cap, so figures on later pages are not proposed |
| D-764 | **Reused: the model-calling code and the document reader.** `selectAdapter` (Anthropic, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `AI_MODEL`), `callModel` (timeout, one retry, failure kinds), `checkAiBudget` (the caller's quota, their address's and the day's ceiling every AI call shares, spent only when the model is asked; 7.4 spent only the day's ceiling because mail has no caller, a click has one), the error codes and the `retryLater` mapping of document-extract, `readJsonCapped`, and document-extract's `readDocument` itself: it is handed an `ask` that keeps the text sections it would have sent and asks this function's model instead, so PDFs, Word files and spreadsheets are read by the code that already reads them, and no second extractor exists. A census-shaped table is mapped by header and never sent to the model. Gap: a scanned PDF with no text is "unreadable" (no OCR) |
| D-765 | **One function, `read-quote-document`: POST `{quote_id}` as the agent's own sign-in.** Row security decides what it can see (the quote, its attachment, the `peo-mail` file); another firm's quote and a missing one are the same 404. A read, so it needs no advisor check and is allowed in a support session or a closing firm (nothing is written; the budget is the only thing spent). It answers 401, 400, 413, 405 (the request), 404, 422 `no_document` (the quote names none), 409 `not_readable` (the file is pending, blocked or not kept: never fetched, never sent to the model), 429 and 503 (the budget, as document-extract) and 502 or 504 (the model failed, by its code). 200 carries `status` (`proposed`; `nothing_found`; `unavailable`; the reader's `encrypted`, `unreadable`, `unsupported`, `too_large`) and `proposal {draft, sources}`. Logs hold ids and kinds only, never the document or the model's words. Gap: no `ai_extraction_events` row as document-extract writes (its columns are for documents of an intake; a quote reading would need its own kind) |
| D-766 | **With no model key nothing is proposed and nothing breaks:** 200 `unavailable` with an empty proposal, one `AI_CONFIG_MISSING` log line, the file not even fetched and no budget spent |
| D-767 | **The API helper, `readQuoteDocument(quoteId)` in `src/lib/advisorApi.ts`,** returns `{status, message, draft, sources}`, where `draft` is `Partial<QuoteOffer>` (it can be spread into `recordQuote(quoteRequestId, { kind, ...draft })` once the broker has chosen `kind`: test 27) and `sources` maps `QuoteFigure` (`kind`, `receivedOn`, `expiresOn`, `startOn`, `adminFee`, `oneOffFees`, `workersComp`, `unemployment`, `medical`) to a page. A refusal throws `AdvisorApiError` in the function's words. **How 7.8's form calls it:** the form saves the quote first only if the broker wants a document read from a hand-typed quote (D-738: record, upload with `uploadQuoteDocument(quoteId, file)`, then read); for a quote that already has its document, a "Read the document" button calls `readQuoteDocument(quote.id)` on click (never on load: each call spends one unit of the day's AI budget), puts each figure of `draft` in its field marked "Suggested", shows `sources[figure]` beside it ("from Page 2"), leaves a figure not in `draft` empty ("Not quoted"), asks for `kind` unless `draft.kind` is set, and saves only through `recordQuote` when the broker confirms. On `unavailable`, `nothing_found` or a refusal it says so in words and leaves the form as it was |
| D-768 | **Registries and words.** No table or database function (D-760). The function joins `scripts/supabase-setup-lib.mjs` (19 functions deployed) and `docs/SUPABASE_SETUP.md`; it imports document-extract's reader, so the SheetJS bundling note there applies (Docker bundling). `dataPolicy.ts` says the AI provider reads a quote document on request, text only, with sensitive numbers removed. `GLOSSARY.md`: **Quote reading** |
| D-769 | **Not built here.** The button and the form fields (7.8); storing which figures were accepted or changed (D-760); OCR; reading past the character cap (D-763); caching a reading. "A quote isn't shown to a prospect until a broker has checked it" holds because nothing shows a quote to a prospect yet (N-3.3) and a proposal is never stored, so it cannot reach one |

## Review of one story

The `code-review` skill, against the epic's branch: Standards, Spec and Adversarial, each read independently,
then validated. Ready means no verified P0 or P1 and no incomplete axis. P2 findings are fixed before merging;
P3 are fixed or listed in the pull request.
